## How to Use This Checklist

Each item names its planned evidence class, the obligations or FCTM rows it supports, and its status. **Every item is `PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED`; no item may be marked PASS** until executed in an authorized environment at its verification stage. Section 14.4 is the row-keyed authority: one item per `IN SCOPE` row.

## Evidence Classes

| Class | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `UNIT` | Unit or component test in the Fast Gate (mocked providers) |
| `INT` | Server-function integration test on the isolated test project with a mocked or recorded provider |
| `DB` | Database-level behavioural test on the isolated test project |
| `CONC` | Concurrency, race, crash or replay test |
| `INV` | Machine-generated effective-access or repository inventory: owners, grants, default privileges, RLS, view semantics, function signatures and overloads, security mode and `search_path`, privileged handlers, manifest completeness |
| `AUDIT` | Inspection of domain events and SB-P-1.12 `authority_events` produced by a test |
| `EVAL` | Governed English/Malayalam/Manglish answer and injection-fixture evaluation against a recorded provider configuration |
| `PERF` | Latency or plan measurement against the recorded target |
| `REH` | Migration rehearsal evidence on the isolated test project (baseline, transition, rollback, forward-fix, restore) |
| `RT` | Human runtime verification in an authorized non-production environment |
| `FRT` | Founder runtime observation of a Founder-reserved scenario |
| `REG` | Regression re-run of existing suites |
| `REPO` | Repository evidence: diff, file inventory, locked documents unchanged |
| `PROV` | Separately authorized provider, runtime or environment evidence; never inferred from files |
| `DOC` | Documentary design review; not implementation or runtime evidence |

## 11. Obsolete 06:00 and 09:15 Schedule Regression (`EIS15-VER-LEG`)

Executed only in an authorized, isolated test environment; never production. Fixture LEG-F1 to LEG-F6 and enforcement points as specified in EIS Section 24.2. Supports `EC15-VER-LEG`, `EC15-SCH-01`, `EC15-MIG-04`; U-3 evidence consumed by SB-P-1.19 (MC-80).

| VC-LEG-B | Part B: staged legacy rows reconciled produce no live rows; one `sched_quarantine` row and one `LEGACY_ARTIFACT_QUARANTINED` event each; valid controls imported; `UNSET` controls quarantined | `DB`, `AUDIT` | PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED |
| VC-LEG-C | Part C: `pg_cron` and platform wake-ups at 06:00 and 09:15 (UTC and IST) create no work and write `WAKEUP_REJECTED`; trigger inventory marks legacy jobs nonconforming; startup refuses legacy configuration | `DB`, `INV`, `AUDIT` | PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED |
| VC-LEG-D | Part D: zero new briefing revisions, notification requests or states, attempts, effects or sends; nothing displays 06:00 or 09:15 as current | `DB`, `RT` | PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED |

## 13. Founder-Reserved Scenarios

Founder Scenarios A and B are reserved to the Founder at Stage 17/18 (`FRT`): RS-01 and RS-02 (Scenario A, `BP-§10.4-A1`, `-A2`), RS-03 to RS-05 (Scenario B, `BP-§10.4-B1` to `-B3`, FDB-3 qualified answer, Manager subset, no write). Each remains PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED.

## 14. Product Truth Coverage and Drift Verification

### 14.1 Classification rules

- **Permission, isolation, privilege and denial row:** FCTM kind `NEG`, or a row realized by any obligation verified by a named negative-path check (Section 3). Each carries at least one named check; every `NEG` row also carries NP-20.
- **Experience row:** FCTM kind `SCN`, `EXP`, `FRV` or `WORK`, or a row realized by a user-visible obligation verified by a runtime scenario (Section 4) or `EC15-UX-*`. Each carries at least one named scenario; where no obligation-specific scenario applies, the scenario for its Blueprint Section 8 home is used.
- **Planned evidence class:** the union of the classes of the row's obligations, negative-path checks and runtime scenarios.
- **Stage 7 status and holds:** carried from the locked EIS; `DEPENDENCY-HELD` and `FOLLOW-UP` rows stay `IN SCOPE`.

| Measure | Value |
|---|---|
| `IN SCOPE` FCTM rows | 694 |
| Rows with a checklist item and planned evidence class | 694 of 694 |
| Rows missing a checklist item | 0 |
| Permission, isolation, privilege and denial rows | 694; all carry a named negative-path check |
| Experience rows | 667; all carry a named runtime scenario |
| Named negative-path checks (NP-01 to NP-24, SR6-01 to SR6-18) | 42, each used by at least one row |
| Named runtime scenarios | 21, each used by at least one row |
| Topic items (Sections 1, 2, 5 to 12) | 45 |
| Items marked PASS | 0 |
| Behaviour-adding orphan items | 0 |

## 15. Verification-Stage Ownership

| Stage | Owner | Scope |
|---|---|---|
| Stage 15–16 (build and builder report) | Authorized builder, when named | Fast Gate and Full Assurance runs; Builder Completion Report per row; never self-acceptance |
| Stage 17 (human runtime verification) | Founder or delegated human verifier | `RT`, `FRT`; Founder Scenarios A and B |
| Stage 18 (Mission Control runtime review) | Mission Control | Runtime evidence review |
| Stage 19 (independent verification) | Non-author independent verifier | `INV`, `DB`, `CONC` security and isolation evidence, including SR6-01 to SR6-18 and `EIS15-VER-LEG` |
| Migration rehearsal | Separately authorized migration mission and operations | `REH` |
| Provider and runtime evidence | Separately authorized | `PROV` |

## 16. Final Acceptance Statement

Not executed. Completed only after the package is locked, a separate Implementation Authorization has been recorded, the work has been performed, and Stages 16 to 19 have produced their records. Mission Control decides acceptance.

```text
CHECKLIST EXECUTION: NOT STARTED
ITEMS: ALL PROPOSED — NOT EXECUTED
ROWS DEMONSTRATED: 0 OF 694
D12: HELD (548 ROWS)
D13: HELD (534 ROWS)
D14: HELD (298 ROWS)
DTX: HELD (33 ROWS)
AI PROVIDER EVIDENCE: NOT ACCEPTED (200 ROWS)
SCHEDULER RUNTIME: NOT SELECTED (268 ROWS)
DC-06: PROPOSED
IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORIZATION: WITHHELD — IMPLEMENTATION NOT AUTHORIZED
```
